# The G-TIME-F0 RESULTS entry and Code's step-10 ping to the seat -- values-check, both passes (before commit).
# Code stamp: 2026-10-13 03:52:11 UTC (date -u). HEAD dac74b7 (the run's outputs); the entry and the ping uncommitted while checked.
# Each pass: three verifiers (the entry's two halves; the ping) extract and check every atomic claim with tools; a STRICT refuter
#   re-checks every FAILS/IMPRECISE; a values lens reads both texts whole. All agents read-only; no model loaded; the pick-bearing
#   checkpoint never opened (instructed); the contrast session's duration never computed (instructed).
# Pass 1: 269 claims (231 verified, 22 imprecise, 1 fails, 15 uncheckable); the refuters CONFIRMED all 23 flags; 19 values findings
#   (3 major). Pass 2: 302 claims (281 verified, 10 imprecise, 11 uncheckable); refuters 9 CONFIRMED, 1 REFUTED; 18 values
#   findings (5 major). Code's disposition: every confirmed correction and every values finding of both passes applied (E48,
#   refuted, not applied as a correction; its companion note V17 applied).
# Pass 1's X2/C61 refuters and pass 2's V13 re-derived step 9's post-commit licence check read-only at HEAD dac74b7 (lock,
#   analyzer and package unchanged): [] for the committed lock; the superseded-analyzer control refused on (d) and (f), the
#   strings as the entry quotes them.

The workflow script (both passes ran it; PASS from args): /Users/jasondwyer/.claude/projects/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/workflows/scripts/gtime-entry-valuescheck-wf_fe21ea79-177.js
export const meta = {
  name: 'gtime-entry-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check the G-TIME-F0 RESULTS entry and the seat ping block: claim verifiers, strict refuters, a values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'three verifiers extract and check every atomic claim' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'a strict refuter per flagged claim batch' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'a values lens over both texts' },
  ],
}

const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const PY = REPO + '/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1

const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code = the build/run role, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Code has written two texts to check BEFORE commit (values-check pass ${PASS}):
 (E) the new top entry of RESULTS.md, "2026-10-12 — G-TIME-F0 ..." (from the line after "## Log — Phase 2" down to the line before the next "- 2026-10-10 — **LOCK-m-F0 v0.1" entry);
 (P) results/raw/2026-10-12_m-code-to-design_G-TIME-F0.md (Code's block to the design seat).
Sources of truth: git history (commits 0fe65b9 driver, dac74b7 run outputs, e3bef6f LOCK-m-F0.md v0.1, earlier); LOCK-m-F0.md; PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (cite "m:N" = line N); the raws results/raw/2026-10-12_m-gtime-f0.txt, _m-gtime-driver_review.txt, _m-gtime-driver_selftest.txt; data/m/reports/Q-F0_time_report.json and _reads.json; scripts/m_sessions.py and valence_core/m/*.py at HEAD; the PI's message (chat, quoted verbatim in both texts -- treat that quote as given, but check that both texts quote it identically and attribute it correctly: the PI's message routes the seat's answer; do not credit the seat with the PI's own sentences or vice versa). Step 9's post-commit licence check is recorded only from Code's session; its stated output can be re-derived by running read-only: ${PY} -I -c with sys.path including ${REPO}, stages.licence_problems on the committed lock (the identity_extra named in E), and the negative control with analyzer 24d44a3c61b06dd3a5e116e2edf59268174ca4e65e9b9960b20873b9d31327fa -- the outputs must match E's quotes (HEAD has moved since, but the lock, analyzer and package are unchanged).
HARD RULES: read-only. Do NOT edit any file. No git command that writes. Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model. NEVER open, print, hash-then-read or parse data/m/ckpt/* (pick-bearing; hashing it with shasum is allowed). Do not compute or state the contrast (A0) session's duration from the raw's stamps (E and P flag that this is inferable; leave it uncomputed).`

const CLAIMS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' },
      verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
      evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
    }, required: ['id', 'text', 'where', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } },
  },
  required: ['claims'],
}
const REF = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['verdicts'],
}
const VALUES = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] },
    where: { type: 'string' }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } },
  required: ['findings'],
}

const PARTS = [
  { key: 'E1', scope: 'text (E) from its first line through the end of the "Launch conditions" bullet (i.e. the headline, stamp, word, driver, review, self-test, step 9 check, launch conditions)' },
  { key: 'E2', scope: 'text (E) from "The run" bullet to its end (the run, identity, bars, reported beside, first pick, timing, picks, outputs table, the ping summary, what follows, the values-check line)' },
  { key: 'P', scope: 'text (P), the whole block (its sections 0-4, its quotes of m:263, m:271, m:280, the lock items, the questions and Code\'s stated defaults and proposals; check the two transcripts in section 1 are byte-identical to data/m/reports/Q-F0_time_reads.json\'s two "transcript" fields)' },
]

const results = await pipeline(
  PARTS,
  p => agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (verifier ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). Extract EVERY atomic factual claim in ${p.scope} -- every number, hash, path, commit, line anchor, quote, attribution, count, date/stamp, and every statement about what code does or what a ruling/word says -- and check each against the sources with tools (git show/log/grep, sha256, reading files, read-only python). Quotes must be verbatim (say exactly where they differ). Attributions must be exact (who said it; whose reading). Verdict per claim: VERIFIED, FAILS (wrong), IMPRECISE (true in part / overstated / misattributed), UNCHECKABLE (say why). Give the evidence and, for FAILS/IMPRECISE, an exact correction. Be exhaustive: a missed claim is a failure of this pass.`,
    { label: `verify:${p.key}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS }),
  (res, p) => {
    const flagged = (res && res.claims ? res.claims : []).filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
    if (flagged.length === 0) return { part: p.key, claims: res ? res.claims : [], refuted: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (STRICT refuter, ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). A verifier flagged the claims below as FAILS or IMPRECISE. For EACH, independently re-check against the sources and try to REFUTE the flag (i.e. show the original claim is in fact right). CONFIRMED = the flag stands (the text must change; give the exact corrected wording, minimal, preserving everything true); REFUTED = the claim was right (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled.
FLAGGED:
${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${p.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
      .then(v => ({ part: p.key, claims: res.claims, refuted: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
const values = await agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (values lens, pass ${PASS}). Read (E) and (P) whole, as the design seat and the PI will. Find what a careful reader would object to that a fact check misses: misattribution of readings (Code's vs the seat's vs the PI's vs the proposal's); any statement that self-adjudicates what is the seat's to decide; any leak against the blinding rules (m:271, m:318, ruling (h): no contrast pick, no contrast free-round class or position, no contrast timing; the A0 stdout line 'excluded@free' is m:263's sanctioned form -- check the texts say no more than that); overclaims ("all", "every", "nothing", "none"); questions to the seat that are not neutrally posed or that pre-decide; missing items the PI's word required (record the driver's new sha256 in the entry; ping with the forced rounds and the lock together); internal inconsistencies between (E) and (P); anything unclear to a reader without Code's session. Give each finding with suggested replacement text.`,
  { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, parts: results, values: values ? values.findings : [] }
